The repo has a bug: some code path fails to unsubscribe a\n`'job:done'` handler, leaking subscribers. Fix it. Keep every public API and\nsignature unchanged; do not delete files; adding files is fine. Node.js\nbuiltins only, ESM, no external packages.\n\n`Bus` provides exactly: `on(type, handler)` (adds, returns `this`), `off(type,\nhandler)` (removes; unknown handler/type ignored; returns `this`),\n`emit(type, payload)`, `listenerCount(type)` (0 for unknown types). The same handler function registered twice on a type counts once.\n`emit` returns the count invoked, runs them in registration order, and\nsnapshots the list when it begins: a handler added during an emit runs only\nin later emits; one removed during an emit still runs later in that emit.\n\n`Scheduler(bus, options)`, `options = {now}`; validate `bus` first, then\n`now`. `bus` must provide `on`, `off`, `emit` as functions, else `TypeError`\n(`'bus must provide on, off and emit'`). `now` must be a function or undefined\n(default `Date.now`), else `TypeError` (`'now must be a function or\nundefined'`). `now` is called exactly: once per successful `start`, once per\njob-transitioning `cancel`, and once by the done handler when a payload lacks\na finite `atMs`. Nothing else invokes it.\n\n`schedule(id, runAtMs)`: validate `id` (non-empty string) then `runAtMs`\n(finite number), else `TypeError`. If the id maps to a non-terminal job throw\n`Error('duplicate job id')`. Creates exactly one `'job:done'` subscription and\nreturns `{id, runAtMs, status: 'pending', startedAt: null, endedAt: null,\ndurationMs: null}`. Rescheduling a terminal id replaces that record.\n\n`start(id)`: only a `pending` job moves to `running` with\n`startedAt = now()`, returning true; any other job returns false unchanged.\n\n`cancel(id)`: unknown or terminal jobs return false. A `pending` or `running`\njob moves to `cancelled`, sets `endedAt = now()`, returns true, and MUST\nunsubscribe its `'job:done'` handler, so a later `emit('job:done', {id})` is a\nno-op: status and `endedAt` stay unchanged, for started and never-started\njobs alike.\n\nEmitting `'job:done'` with payload `{id, atMs}` moves a non-terminal job with\nthat id to `done`, sets `endedAt = atMs` (or `now()` if `atMs` is not finite),\nunsubscribes, and sets `durationMs = endedAt - startedAt` if started, else\nnull. Terminal jobs, other ids and non-object payloads are ignored.\n\n`status(id)` returns the status string or null. `snapshot()` returns a fresh\narray of fresh view objects each call. `stats()` returns counts:\n`{total, pending, running, done, cancelled}`.\n\n\n\nNote: aim for a clean, readable implementation; correctness matters most."}],"timestamp":1794045887138}}

Exported API: class Scheduler.\n// See behavior.md for the invariants this module must hold.\nimport { Bus } from './bus.js';\n\nconst DONE = 'job:done';\n\nexport class Scheduler {\n  constructor(bus, options = {}) {\n    if (!bus || typeof bus.on !== 'function' || typeof bus.off !== 'function' || typeof bus.emit !== 'function') {\n      throw new TypeError('bus must provide on, off and emit');\n    }\n    if (options.now !== undefined && typeof options.now !== 'function') {\n      throw new TypeError('now must be a function or undefined');\n    }\n    this._bus = bus;\n    this._now = options.now === undefined ? Date.now : options.now;\n    /** @type {Map<string, any>} */\n    this._jobs = new Map();\n  }\n\n  schedule(id, runAtMs) {\n    if (typeof id !== 'string' || id.length === 0) {\n      throw new TypeError('id must be a non-empty string');\n    }\n    if (typeof runAtMs !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(runAtMs)) {\n      throw new TypeError('runAtMs must be a finite number');\n    }\n    if (this._jobs.has(id)) {\n      throw new Error('duplicate job id');\n    }\n    const job = {\n      id,\n      runAtMs,\n      status: 'pending',\n      startedAt: null,\n      endedAt: null,\n    };\n    const handler = (event) => {\n      if (!event || typeof event !== 'object') return;\n      if (event.id !== id) return;\n      job.status = 'done';\n      job.endedAt = Number.isFinite(event.atMs) ? event.atMs : this._now();\n      this._bus.off(DONE, handler);\n    };\n    job._handler = handler;\n    this._jobs.set(id, job);\n    this._bus.on(DONE, handler);\n    return this._view(job);\n  }\n\n  start(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    if (!job || job.status !== 'pending') return false;\n    job.status = 'running';\n    job.startedAt = this._now();\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  cancel(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    if (!job) return false;\n    if (job.status === 'done' || job.status === 'cancelled') return false;\n    if (job.startedAt === null) {\n      // Nothing has started yet: just mark it cancelled.\n      job.status = 'cancelled';\n      job.endedAt = this._now();\n      return true;\n    }\n    this._bus.off(DONE, job._handler);\n    job.status = 'cancelled';\n    job.endedAt = this._now();\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  status(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    return job ? job.status : null;\n  }\n\n  _view(job) {\n    const durationMs =\n      job.status === 'done' && job.startedAt !== null && job.endedAt !== null\n        ? job.endedAt - job.startedAt\n        : null;\n    return {\n      id: job.id,\n      runAtMs: job.runAtMs,\n      status: job.status,\n      startedAt: job.startedAt,\n      endedAt: job.endedAt,\n      durationMs,\n    };\n  }\n\n  snapshot() {\n    return [...this._jobs.values()].map((job) => this._view(job));\n  }\n\n  stats() {\n    const counts = { total: 0, pending: 0, running: 0, done: 0, cancelled: 0 };\n    for (const job of this._jobs.values()) {\n      counts.total++;\n      counts[job.status]++;\n    }\n    return counts;\n  }\n}\n"}],"details":{}}}

For a **pending** (never-started) job, the code took an early-return branch that marked the job `cancelled` **without** unsubscribing its `'job:done'` handler — leaking the subscriber. A later `emit('job:done', {id})` would then fire and incorrectly flip the job to `done`.\n\nThe fix unsubscribes the handler in all cancellation cases (both pending and running), matching the spec: *\"A `pending` or `running` job moves to `cancelled` ... and MUST unsubscribe its `'job:done'` handler.\"*\n\nThe merged cancel logic is now:\n```js\ncancel(id) {\n  const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n  if (!job) return false;\n  if (job.status === 'done' || job.status === 'cancelled') return false;\n  this._bus.off(DONE, job._handler);\n  job.status = 'cancelled';\n  job.endedAt = this._now();\n  return true;\n}\n```\n\nAll public APIs, signatures, validation, and other behavior are unchanged."}],"api":"anthropic-messages","provider":"bench","model":"deepseek-v4-flash","usage":{"input":118,"output":256,"cacheRead":5376,"cacheWrite":0,"totalTokens":5750,"cost":{"input":0,"output":0,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0}},"stopReason":"stop","timestamp":1794045895146,"responseId":"msg_3f7aab88049047bfa6b741f0"}]}
